- **forgemaster**: Judge the complexity of the request, **route** to the right-sized Forge path, then run it gated — pausing at each boundary for your approval. Medium-or-higher work runs the full four-phase pipeline (**research → plan → implement → ship**); low-complexity work is handed to the trimmed `forge-mini` path to save turns and cost. You stay in control at every step. ([jupes/agent-forge-harness](https://github.com/jupes/agent-forge-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/forgemaster.md) · ★2)
- **forgemaster-auto**: Same four phases as `/forgemaster` — **research → plan → implement → ship** — but every phase reviews its own output with a **fresh evaluator subagent**, feeds the findings back, revises, and **advances itself**. No `AskUserQuestion`, no approval gates in the happy path. ([jupes/agent-forge-harness](https://github.com/jupes/agent-forge-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/forgemaster-auto.md) · ★2)
- **forgemaster-mini**: Run the **low-ceremony** Forge path for small, clear-scope work (bugs, chores, config, single-file changes). Same Beads + TDD discipline as the full pipeline, but collapsed into three lightweight steps — scope → build → wrap — with far fewer turns, gates, and artifacts to keep cost down. ([jupes/agent-forge-harness](https://github.com/jupes/agent-forge-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/forgemaster-mini.md) · ★2)

- **fp-docs:setup**: Initialize or verify the fp-docs plugin installation. Checks plugin structure, docs repo setup, codebase gitignore, and branch sync state. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/setup.md))
- **fp-docs:sync**: Synchronize the docs repo branch with the codebase branch. Creates or switches docs branches, generates diff reports, and optionally merges docs branches. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/sync.md))
- **fp-docs:test**: Execute runtime validations against the local development environment. Tests REST endpoints, WP-CLI commands, template rendering, and visual verification via browser automation. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/test.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-audit**: Audit user guide coverage gaps. Compare user-visible features in codebase against documented pages. Report undocumented features. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-audit.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-batch**: Batch operations across multiple user guide pages. Uses Agent Teams for parallel execution when enabled, falls back to sequential. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-batch.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-generate**: Generate a new user guide page for a feature or workflow. Analyzes code, discovers UI paths, captures screenshots, and writes content using the appropriate page template. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-generate.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-preview**: Start local Hugo dev server or trigger preview deploy for user guide on current branch. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-preview.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-screenshot**: Capture or refresh screenshots for user guide pages. Uses Playwright MCP to navigate WP admin and capture current UI state. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-screenshot.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-status**: Report user guide health metrics. Shows page counts, screenshot inventory, coverage percentage, staleness, and structural health. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-status.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-update**: Update an existing user guide page after codebase changes. Detects what changed, re-verifies UI paths, and refreshes stale screenshots. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-update.md))
- **fp-docs:ug-validate**: Validate user guide accuracy against current UI state. Checks navigation paths, screenshot currency, tone compliance, and content completeness. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/ug-validate.md))
- **fp-docs:update**: Check for and install fp-docs plugin updates. Shows changelog from GitHub release notes, confirms before updating. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/update.md))
- **fp-docs:update-claude**: Regenerate the CLAUDE.md template with current skill inventory, documentation links, and project configuration. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/update-claude.md))
- **fp-docs:update-index**: Refresh the PROJECT-INDEX.md codebase reference. Scans source files and updates the master index. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/update-index.md))
- **fp-docs:update-skills**: Regenerate all plugin skills from the current prompt definitions. Syncs skill files with the source-of-truth prompts. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/update-skills.md))
- **fp-docs:verbosity-audit**: Scan existing documentation for verbosity gaps — missing items, summarization language, unexpanded enumerables. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/verbosity-audit.md))
- **fp-docs:verify**: Run the 10-point verification checklist on documentation files without making changes. Reports pass/fail for each check. ([tomkyser/fp-docs](https://github.com/tomkyser/fp-docs/blob/HEAD/commands/verify.md))
